CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
China Modern Dairy Holdings Limited (the “Company” or
“Modern Dairy” together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is
the leading dairy farming operator and fresh raw milk producer
in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). Modern Dairy
operates 33 dairy farming companies in the PRC with dairy
cows over 350,000 heads and annualised milk yield of over
2 million tons. The Group is a global leading dairy farming
operator and raw milk producer in terms of herd size. The
Company initiated the production model of “Zero distance
Integration of Forage Grass Planting, Cows Breeding and Milk
Processing within Two Hours”, which is the first in the PRC that
applies integrated production model with SGS certification.
Modern Dairy’s products have been awarded the Gold Prize
of Monde Selection which is generally regarded as the “Nobel
Prize” in the food industry, for the past eight consecutive years.

中國現代牧業控股有限公司（ 「本公司」或「現代牧業」連同其附
屬公司「本集團」），為中華人民共和國（
                  「中國」）乳牛畜牧營運商
及原料奶生產商的翹楚。現代牧業於中國經營33家乳牛牧場公
司，擁有逾35萬頭乳牛，年化產奶量達200萬噸以上。本公司
首創「種養加一體化、零距離2小時」的生產模式，是中國首家
一體化生產模式並通過SGS認證的企業。現代牧業的產品於過
去八年連續在素有食品業「諾貝爾獎」之稱的世界食品品質評鑒
大會中獲得金獎。
       “Farming diligently for the purity,
       		 genuineness, freshness and vitality of fresh milk”
                               「為牛乳的純、真、鮮、活，而默默耕耘」

MEMORIAL AND GRATITUDE                                                銘記昨天•感恩同行

The year 2021 was a landmark year.                                    剛剛過去的2021年，是具有里程碑意義的一年。

For our country, the year 2021 was of great significance. We          回首2021年，於國家而言，意義非凡。我們共同
experienced the historical intersection of the “Two Centenary         經歷了「兩個一百年」奮鬥目標歷史交匯，見證了
Goals” and witnessed the good start of China’s “14th Five-Year        國家「十四五」的良好開局。
Plan”.

Looking back at 2021, it was of great significance to our industry.   回望這一歲，於行業而言，意義深刻。我們一起
We started a new journey for the development of the dairy industry    開啓了「十四五」奶業發展的新征程，明確了中國
in the “14th Five-Year Plan” and clarified the new directions to      奶業開往現代化奶業的新座標。

Quality Management                                                        質量管理
The overall execution of the quality management of Modern Dairy           現代牧業質量控制整體執行「四不」原則：不合格
is based on the principle of “Four Don’ts”: don’t cooperate with          的客戶不合作、到場不合格的原料不入場、庫存
unqualified client, don’t stock unqualified raw feed materials, don’t     不合格的原飼料不投餵、檢測不合格的原奶不出
feed with unqualified inventory of raw feed materials, and don’t          場。
release raw milk from farms that fail inspection.

For the quality control of raw feed materials, the Group ensures          在原飼料質量控制方面，通過供應商現場審核、
the source control by inspecting suppliers on-site and establishing       建立供應商名單庫確保源頭控制；通過到場原料
a database of suppliers; ensures feed arrival control by having           檢測標準嚴於國標確保到場控制；通過每日對庫
a stricter standard of testing than that of the national standard;        存原料抽檢、指標監控確保過程控制；通過使用
ensures the process control by sampling the inventory of raw              近紅外精準提供配方參考數據、生產過程中實時

Corporate Governance Functions                                               企業管治職能
The Board is responsible for determining the policies for corporate          董事會負責釐定本公司之企業管治政策及履行企
governance of the Company and performing the corporate                       業管治職責，其中包括：
governance duties including:

(a)   to develop and review the Company’s policies and practices             (a)   制定及檢討本公司之企業管治政策及常規；
      on corporate governance;

Nomination Policy                                                      提名政策
The Company has adopted a nomination policy for directorship           本 公 司 於 2018年 已 採 納 董 事 提 名 政 策（「提 名 政
(the “Nomination Policy”) in 2018 which supplemental to the            策」），補充董事會提名委員會（         「提名委員會」     ）職
Terms of Reference of the Nomination Committee of the Board (the       權範圍。其旨在列明提名本公司董事候選人之程
“Nomination Committee”). It aims at setting out the processes          序及標準，並確保董事會成員的所有提名屬公平
and criteria for the nomination of a candidate for directorship in     及透明，從而促進構建董事會，以平衡符合本公
the Company and ensures that all nominations of Board members          司業務規定的技能、經驗及多元發展。
are fair and transparent in order to facilitate the constitution
of the Board with a balance of skills, experience and diversity
of perspectives that is appropriate to the requirements of the
Company’s business.

 AUDITOR’S REMUNERATION                                                核數師酬金
 The Group’s external auditors are Deloitte Touche Tohmatsu. The       本集團的外聘核數師為德勤•關黃陳方會計師行。
 Audit Committee is responsible for the appointment of external        審核委員會負責委任外聘核數師及審閱外聘核數

Risk Management System                                                  風險管理系統
The Group adopts a risk management system which manages                 本集團採納的風險管理系統管理與其業務及營運
the risk associated with its business and operations. The system        相關的風險。該系統包括以下階段：
comprises the following phases:

•    Identification: Identify ownership of risks, business objectives   •   識別：識別風險歸屬、業務目標及可能影響
     and risks that could affect the achievement of objectives.             目標實現的風險。

•    Evaluation: Analyze the likelihood and impact of risks and         •   評估：分析風險的可能性及影響，並據此評
     evaluate the risk portfolio accordingly.                               估風險組合。

•    Management: Consider the risk responses, ensure effective          •   管理：考慮風險回應措施，確保與董事會有
     communication to the Board and on-going monitoring of the              效溝通並持續監控剩餘風險。
     residual risks.

The Board together with senior management monitor and assess            董事會連同高級管理層定期監控及評估風險，推
risk regularly, boost related management standards and evaluate         進相關管理標準及評估投資項目。基於就回顧年
investment projects. Based on the risk assessments conducted for        度所作風險評估，並無識別重大風險。

 Employee Partnership Program                                          員工合夥人計劃
 As the Company’s business scale continues to expand, operating        隨著本公司業務規模的不斷擴展，經營管理及激
 management and incentive system shall keep up with the times.         勵制度需要與時俱進，傳統的「制度管理+目標實
 The traditional rationale of management by objectives has             現」的管理有其局限性。因此，如何激發員工積極
 limitations. How to ignite employees’ working enthusiasm to serve     服務公司、增強員工主人翁意識、與公司共同發
 the company proactively, and enhance their sense of ownership         展，是公司需要深入考慮的。為此，本公司在現
 and strive for the development of the company together are the        有限股獎勵計劃外，特別制定了員工合夥人盈利
 topics that the Company needs to cope with in depth. Therefore,       分享激勵計劃（「員工合夥人計劃」
                                                                                      ），以打造員工
 on top of the existing restricted share award schemes, the            與公司的利益共同體。
 Company has specially formulated an employee partnership profit-
 sharing program (the “Employee Partnership Program”) with aim
 to build a community of shared interests amongst the Company
 and its employees.

 Many leading enterprises in mainland China realized that the          眾多國內龍頭企業也意識到合夥人制度與公司的
 partnership systems are closely correlated to a sustainable           長遠發展是息息相關的，因此，紛紛推行合夥人
 development of the company, and start to implement the                管理模式，讓員工與公司形成利益共同體、事業
 partnership management system one after another, allowing             共同體、命運的共同體。
 employees and company to build a community of shared interests,
 business and future.

 Operational Risks                                                        營運風險
 Operational risk is concerned with possible losses caused by failure     營運風險與內部程序失效、人員、制度或外部事
 of internal processes, people, system or external events, such           項造成的可能損失，此風險透過建立健全的內部
 risk is mitigated by and controlled by establishing sound internal       監控、明確責任範圍、適當的職責分離及有效的
 control, setting out clear lines of responsibility, proper segregation   內部報告予以降低及控制。管理層定期檢討本集
 of duties and effective internal reporting. Management regularly         團營運以確保本集團虧損（不論是財務或其他方
 reviews the Group’s operations to ensure that the Group’s risk of        面）、欺詐所導致、錯誤、疏忽或其他營運及合規
 losses, whether financial or otherwise, resulting from fraud, errors,    事宜的風險得到充分管理。
 omissions and other operational and compliance matters, are
 adequately managed.

 Financial Risks                                                          財務風險

 regular reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures            貨幣遠期合約或貨幣期權合約（如需要），以管理
 and may enter into currency forward contracts or currency option         其外匯風險。此外，為應對利率波動的風險，管
 contracts, when necessary, to manage its foreign exchange                理層定期檢討及評估本集團的利率變動風險，並
 exposure. Furthermore, with aim to tackle the risk of interest rate      可考慮在必要時訂立利率掉期合約或其他對沖策
 fluctuation, the management regularly reviews and assesses the           略。
 Group’s exposure to interest rate movement and may consider
 entering into interest rate swap contracts or other hedging
 strategies when necessary.

            Revenue from contracts with customers                                      客戶合約收入
            T h e G r o u p r e c o g n i s e s r e v e n u e w h e n (o r a s) a      於或當本集團達成履約責任時確認收
            performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control”                   入，即當與特定履約責任相關的商品
            of the goods or services underlying the particular                         或服務的「控制權」轉移予客戶時。
            performance obligation is transferred to the customer.

            A performance obligation represents a good or service                       履約責任指可區分的單一商品或服務
            (or a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a                   （或組合商品或服務）或大致相同的一
            series of distinct goods or services that are substantially                 系列可區分商品或服務。
            the same.

            Control is transferred over time and revenue is                            倘滿足以下其中一項標準，則控制權
            recognised over time by reference to the progress                          隨著時間而轉移，並參考完全達成相
            towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                              關履約責任的進度隨著時間確認收
            performance obligation if one of the following criteria is                 入：
            met:

            •      the customer simultaneously receives and                            •   客戶同時收取並使用本集團履約
                   consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                           所帶來的利益；
                   performance as the Group performs;

            •      the Group’s performance creates and enhances                        •   本集團的履約增設並增加於本集
                   an asset that the customer controls as the Group                        團履約時客戶所控制的資產；或
                   performs; or

            •      the Group’s performance does not create an                          •   本集團的履約並無增設對本集團
                   asset with an alternative use to the Group and the                      有額外用途的資產，而本集團有
                   Group has an enforceable right to payment for                           強制執行權利收取迄今已完成的
                   performance completed to date.                                          履約付款。

            Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time                        否則，收入於客戶取得可區分商品或
            when the customer obtains control of the distinct good                     服務的控制權的時點確認。
            or service.

            A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation                     合約負債指本集團有義務將其已向客
            to transfer goods or services to a customer for which                      戶收取代價（或到期的代價金額）的商
            the Group has received consideration (or an amount of                      品或服務轉移至客戶的責任。
            consideration is due) from the customer.

        Definition of a lease                                              租賃的定義
        A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys        倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一
        the right to control the use of an identified asset for a          時期內控制使用已識別資產，則該合
        period of time in exchange for consideration.                      約屬於租賃或包含租賃。

        For contracts entered into or modified on or after the             就於初次應用國際財務報告準則第16

        business combinations, the Group assesses whether a                產生的合約而言，本集團根據國際財
        contract is or contains a lease based on the definition            務報告準則第16號於開始、修訂日
        under IFRS 16 at inception, modification date or                   期或收購日期（視何者合適而定）的定
        acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will               義評估合約是否屬於或包含租賃。除
        not be reassessed unless the terms and conditions                  非合約條款及條件其後改變，否則不
        of the contract are subsequently changed. As a                     得重新評估該合約。作為可行權宜方
        practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics           法，當本集團合理預期對綜合財務報
        are accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group                  表的影響與投資組合內的個別租賃並

        financial statements would not differ materially from              按組合基準入賬。
        individual leases within the portfolio.

        months or less from the commencement date and                      設備及土地租賃。其亦就低價值資產
        do not contain a purchase option. It also applies the              租賃應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低價
        recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets.               值資產租賃的租賃付款於租期內以直

        term.

            Income tax expenses represent the sum of the tax                  所得稅支出指即期應付稅項及遞延稅
            currently payable and deferred tax.                               項總和。

            The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for          即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計
            the year. Taxable profit differs from profit (loss) before        算。由於其他年度的應課稅或可扣稅
            tax because of income or expense that are taxable                 收入或開支及毋須課稅或不可扣稅的
            or deductible in other years and items that are never             項目，故應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利（虧
            taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current          損）不同。本集團的即期稅項負債按報

            or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting              算。
            period.

            Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences               遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資產及負
            between the carrying amount of assets and liabilities             債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所用相應
            in the consolidated financial statements and the                  稅基之間的暫時差額確認。遞延稅項
            corresponding tax bases used in the computation                   資產一般按可能有應課稅溢利用作扣
            of taxable profit. Deferred tax assets are generally              減可動用可扣減暫時差額為限，就所
            recognised for all deductible temporary differences to            有該等可扣減暫時差額確認。倘若暫
            the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will          時差額由不影響應課稅溢利或會計溢
            be available against which those deductible temporary             利的交易中初次確認（業務合併除外）
            differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets             資產及負債所產生，則不會確認該等
            and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary               遞延稅項資產及負債。此外，倘暫時
            difference arises from the initial recognition (other than        差額乃因初次確認商譽而產生，亦不
            in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a         會確認遞延稅項負債。
            transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the
            accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are
            not recognised if the temporary difference arises from
            the initial recognition of goodwill.

        carrying amount of the relevant CGU or group of CGUs.             生單位組別之賬面值作比較。

        Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less               可收回金額為公平值減出售成本及使
        costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in         用價值兩者中的較高者。評估使用價
        use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to            值時，估計未來現金流量按除稅前貼
        their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that            現率貼現至其現值，而該貼現率反映
        reflects current market assessments of the time value of          當時市場對貨幣時間值及該項資產（或
        money and the risks specific to the asset (or a CGU) for          現金產生單位）（其未來現金流量的估
        which the estimates of future cash flows have not been            計尚未經調整）特殊風險的評估。
        adjusted.

   3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                      3.2 重要會計政策（續）
        Inventories                                                         存貨
        Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                 存貨乃按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的
        realisable value. Costs of inventories are determined               較低者列賬。存貨成本以先進先出或
        on first-in, first-out or weighted average method. Net              加權平均法釐定。可變現淨值指存貨
        realisable value represents the estimated selling price             估計售價減所有估計完成成本及出售
        for inventories less all estimated costs of completion              所需成本。進行銷售所需的成本包括
        and costs necessary to make the sale. Costs necessary               直接歸屬於銷售的增量成本及本集團
        to make the sale include incremental costs directly                 為進行銷售而必須產生的非增量成
        attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs which            本。
        the Group must incur to make the sale.

        Provisions                                                          撥備
        Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present              倘本集團因過往事件而須承擔現時責
        obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past            任（法律或推定），而本集團可能須履
        event, it is probable that the Group will be required to            行該責任且該責任之金額能可靠地估
        settle that obligation, and a reliable estimate can be              計，則確認撥備。
        made of the amount of the obligation.

        When some or all of the economic benefits required to               當清償撥備所需的部分或全部經濟利
        settle a provision are expected to be recovered from a              益預期可從第三方收回時，如果幾乎
        third party, a receivable is recognised as an asset if it is        可以肯定會收到償付並且應收款的金
        virtually certain that reimbursement will be received and           額能夠可靠地計量，則應收款確認為
        the amount of the receivable can be measured reliably.              一項資產。

        Contingent liabilities                                              或然負債
        A contingent liability is a present obligation arising              或然負債是指因過去事項而產生的現
        from past events but is not recognised because it is                時義務，但因履行該義務不太可能需
        not probable that an outflow of resources embodying                 要體現經濟利益的資源流出或該義務
        economic benefits will be required to settle the                    的金額不能足夠可靠地計量，故不予
        obligation or the amount of the obligation cannot be                確認。
        measured with sufficient reliability.

            and an assessment of both the current conditions at           日期當時狀況及未來狀況預測的評估
            the reporting date as well as the forecast of future          作出調整。
            conditions.

            receivables.                                                  預期信貸虧損。

            allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has             損撥備等於12個月預期信貸虧損，除

        (v)  Measurement and recognition of ECL                           (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
             The measurement of ECL is a function of the                      預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約
             probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the             概率、違約虧損率（即違約時的
             magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                 虧損程度）及違約風險承擔。違
             the exposure at default. The assessment of the                   約概率及違約虧損率的評估乃基
             probability of default and loss given default is                 於過往數據及前瞻性資料作出。
             based on historical data and forward-looking                     預期信貸虧損的預估反映無偏概
             information. Estimation of ECL reflects an                       率加權金額，以各自發生違約的
             unbiased and probability-weighted amount that                    風險為權重釐定。
             is determined with the respective risks of default
             occurring as the weights.

             Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                 一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據
             contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                 合約應付本集團的所有合約現金
             in accordance with the contract and the cash flows               流量與本集團預期收取的現金流
             that the Group expects to receive, discounted                    量（按於初始確認時釐定的實際
             at the effective interest rate determined at initial             利率貼現）之間的差額。該等資
             recognition. The ECL on these assets are assessed                產的預期信貸虧損通過適當分組
             individually or collectively using collective                    的集體評估單獨或集體評估。
             assessment with appropriate groupings.

             For collective assessment, the Group takes into                  對於集體評估，本集團在制定分
             consideration the following characteristics when                 組時考慮了以下特點：
             formulating the grouping:

             •     Past-due status;                                           •    逾期狀態；

             •     Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                  •    債務人的性質、規模及行
                                                                                   業；及

             •     External credit ratings where available.                   •    可用的外部信用評級。

        or loss is recognised in profit or loss on the purchase,              買、出售、發行或註銷本公司本身的

        instruments.

        FVTPL.                                                                量。

      Assumptions were used in the value-in-use calculation of each                   各現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別的使用
      CGU or group of CGUs. The following describes each key                          價值計算採用了假設。以下描述了管理層為
      assumptions the management has based on in preparing the                        進行減值測試而編製現金流量預測時所依據
      cash flow projections for the purpose of impairment test:                       的各項關鍵假設：

      •        The estimated future market price of raw milk, the                     •   原料奶的估計未來市場價格、每個泌
               estimated average daily milk yield at each lactation                       乳週期的估計平均日產奶量及奶牛養
               cycle and estimated feed costs per kg of raw milk for                      殖每公斤原料奶的估計飼料成本；及
               dairy farming; and the estimated revenue and the                           飼料的估計收入及估計成本 – 該等假
               estimate costs for feeds – such assumptions are based                      設基於單位或現金產生單位組別過去
               on the unit’s or group of CGUs’ past performance                           的業績及管理層對市場發展的預期。
               and management’s expectations for the market
               development.

      •        Discount rates – the discount rates used are before tax                •   貼現率 – 所使用的貼現率是稅前並反
               and reflect specific risks relating to the relevant CGU or                 映與相關現金產生單位或現金產生單
               group of CGUs.                                                             位組別相關的特定風險。

      •        Growth rates – The growth rates used to extrapolate the                •   增長率 – 用於推斷自2022年起超過5年
               cash flows beyond the five-year period from 2022 are                       的現金流量增長率是基於每個單位的
               based on the estimated growth rate of each unit, taking                    估計增長率，並已考慮每個現金產生
               into account the industry growth rate, past experience                     單位或現金產生單位組別的行業增長
               and the medium or long term growth target of each                          率、過往經驗及中長期增長目標。
               CGU or group of CGUs.

    The Group is exposed to fair value risks arising from changes       本集團要承受原料奶價格變動帶來的公平值
    in price of raw milk. The Group does not anticipate that            風險。據本集團預計，在可預見的將來原料
    the price of the raw milk will decline significantly in the         奶價格不會大幅下跌。董事認為，本集團並
    foreseeable future and the Directors are of the view that there     無可用的衍生工具或其他可以訂立的合約用
    is no available derivative or other contracts which the Group       於管理原料奶價格下跌的風險。
    can enter into to manage the risk of a decline in the price of
    the raw milk.

    In general, the heifers are inseminated with semen when             一般情況下，小母牛於長至約十四個月大時
    heifers reached approximately 14 months old. After an               進行配種。約九個月的孕期過後，小牛出
    approximately nine months’ pregnancy term, a calf is born           生，奶牛開始生產原料奶及哺乳期開始。奶
    and the dairy cow begins to produce raw milk and the                牛通常會擠奶約340天，之後進入約60天的
    lactation period begins. A milkable cow is typically milked for     乾乳期。
    approximately 340 days before a dry period of approximately
    60 days.

    When a heifer begins to produce raw milk, it is transferred         當小母牛開始產原料奶時，乃按轉撥日期的
    to the category of milkable cows based on the estimated fair        估計公平值轉撥至奶牛類別。
    value on the date of transfer.

    The Group is exposed to a number of risks related to its            本集團面臨與其生物資產有關的多種風險。
    biological assets. The Group is exposed to the following            本集團面臨以下運營風險：
    operating risks:

    i.    Regulatory and environmental risks                            i.    法規及環境風險
          The Group is subject to laws and regulations in the                 本集團須遵守其開展農場種植及育種
          location in which it operates plantation and breeding.              活動所在地的法律及法規。本集團已
          The Group has established environmental policies                    制定旨在遵守當地環境及其他法律的
          and procedures aimed at compliance with local                       環境政策及程序。管理層進行定期檢
          environmental and other laws. Management performs                   查以識別環境風險及確保現有系統足
          regular reviews to identify environmental risks and                 以管理該等風險。
          to ensure that the systems in place are adequate to
          manage these risks.

    ii.   Climate, disease and other natural risks                      ii.   氣候、疾病及其他自然風險
          The Group’s biological assets are exposed to the risk               本集團的生物資產面臨遭受氣候變
          of damage from climatic changes, diseases and other                 化、疾病及其他自然因素損害的風
          natural forces. The Group has extensive processes in                險。本集團已擴展監察及減輕該等風
          place aimed at monitoring and mitigating those risks,               險的程序，包括定期檢查及疾病控制
          including regular inspections and disease controls and              與調查及保險。
          surveys and insurance.

33. RETIREMENT BENEFITS PLANS                                       33. 退休福利計劃
   The PRC employees of the Group are eligible members of             本集團的中國僱員為中國政府營辦的國家管
   the state-managed retirement benefit schemes operated              理退休福利計劃的合資格成員。本公司於中
   by the government of the PRC. The employees of the PRC             國的附屬公司的員工須按員工養老保險及其
   subsidiaries of the company are required to contribute in          他險金的要求每月向國家管理退休福利計劃
   accordance with employee pension insurance and other               作出供款以撥付有關福利。本集團於國家管
   insurance benefits requirements on monthly basis to the            理退休福利計劃的唯一責任為根據有關計劃
   state-managed retirement benefit schemes to fund the               作出規定的供款。
   benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect to the
   state-managed retirement benefit schemes is to make the
   required contributions under the schemes.

 38. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              38. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團的資金管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
      the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 的持續經營能力，同時透過優化債務及股本
      while maximising the return to stakeholders through the               結餘為利益相關者謀求最大回報。本集團的
      optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s              整體策略較去年保持不變。
      overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

      The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which        本集團的資本架構由債務淨額（包括銀行借
      includes bank borrowings, other borrowings and long term              款、其他借款及長期債券（分別披露於附註
      bonds as disclosed in Notes 26, 27 and 28 respectively, net           26、27及28），扣除銀行結餘及現金）及本公
      of bank balances and cash and equity attributable to owners           司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份
      of the Company, comprising issued share capital, share                溢價及儲備）組成。
      premium and reserves.

      The Group’s management reviews the capital structure                  本集團的管理層定期檢討資本架構。作為檢
      on a regular basis. As part of the review, the management             討的一環，管理層會考慮資本成本及各類資
      considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with           本涉及的風險。本集團透過發行新股份及發
      each class of capital. The Group will balance its overall capital     行新債務或贖回現有債務等方式保持整體資
      structure through the issues of new shares and the issue of           本架構的平衡。
      new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

             approach in IFRS 9 to measure the loss allowance at lifetime                                   際財務報告準則第9號內的簡化方法，
             ECL. The Group performs impairment assessment under ECL                                        按全期預期信貸虧損計量虧損撥備。本
             model on trade receivables individually or collectively using                                  集團在預期信貸虧損模型下對應收貿易
             collective assessment with appropriate groupings, of which                                     賬款進行個別或通過適當分組的集體評
             these receivables are grouped collectively based on shared                                     估，其中該等應收款項按照共同的信貸
             credit risk characteristics by reference to ageing for the                                     風險特徵，參照客戶賬齡進行集體分
             customers.                                                                                     組。

 42. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                               42. 公平值計量
      Fair value of the Group’s biological assets, financial                 按經常性基準以公平值或公平值減銷售
      assets and financial liabilities that are measured at fair             成本計量的本集團生物資產、金融資產
      value or fair value less cost to sell on a recurring basis             及金融負債的公平值
      The Group’s biological assets are measured at fair value               本集團的生物資產按公平值減出售成本計
      less costs to sell, other financial assets, equity investment at       量，其他金融資產、按公平值計入其他全面
      FVTOCI and derivative financial instruments are measured at            收入的權益投資及衍生金融工具於各報告期
      fair values at the end of each reporting period. The Directors         末按公平值計量。董事已成立估值團隊，由
      have set up a valuation team, which is headed up by the                本公司首席財務官領導，以釐定適當的估值
      chief financial officer of the Company, to determine the               技術及公平值計量的輸入數據。
      appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value
      measurements.

      In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group     估計資產或負債的公平值時，本集團使用可
      uses market observable data to the extent it is available.             用市場可觀察數據。倘第一級及第二級的輸
      Where Level 1 and Level 2 inputs are not available, the Group          入數據不可用，本集團會委聘第三方合資格
      engages third party qualified valuers to perform the valuation.        估值師進行估值。估值團隊與合資格外聘估
      The valuation team works closely with the qualified external           值師緊密合作，為模型確立合適的估值方法
      valuers to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and          及輸入數據。首席財務官每半年向董事呈報
      inputs to the model. The chief financial officer reports the           估值團隊的發現以闡釋資產及負債公平值波
      valuation team’s findings to the Directors semi-annually to            動的原因。
      explain the cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets
      and liabilities.

 49. EVENTS AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                                    49. 報告期後事項
      On 15 March 2022, Modern Farming entered into an equity                                   於2022年3月15日，現代牧業（集團）與若干
      transfer agreement with certain sellers (the “Sellers”)                                   賣方（ 「賣方」 ）及內蒙古愛養牛科技有限公司
      and Inner Mongolia Aiyangniu Technology Co., Ltd.                                        （
                                                                                               「愛養牛」   ）訂立股權轉讓協議，據此，賣方
      (the “Aiyangniu”), pursuant to which the Sellers agreed                                   同意出售而現代牧業（集團）同意購買愛養牛
      to sell, and Modern Farming agreed to purchase, 75%                                       的75%股權，總代價為人民幣287,774,900
      equity interests in Aiyangniu at a total consideration of                                 元。上述交易構成本公司的關連交易，根據
      RMB287,774,900. The above transaction which constituted                                   上市規則應予以披露，並已於本公司日期為
      a connected transaction of the Company and should be                                      2022年3月15日的公告中公佈。
      disclosed under Listing Rules has been published in the
